Also use for Chinese requests like “跨区域迁移”, “MGC/SMS 迁移流程”, or “迁移排障”. ([binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills](https://github.com/binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills/tree/HEAD/AI-Assist-Migration/Datacenter-on-premise-migration/Vmware-Migration/mgc-cross-region-migration/assets/bundles/2026-04-22-mx2-to-santiago) · ★14 · 5 copies)
- **mgd-acceptance**: Assess acceptance criteria for a concrete MGD game change or reconcile evidence for the Director. Diff/PR reviews use code-review; ordinary implementation completion does not automatically require another handoff. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-acceptance))
- **mgd-art**: Create or visually revise MGD characters, hazards, parcels, backgrounds, UI graphics or animation/gallery variants using scoped art references. Technical integration of unchanged images and gameplay/hitbox changes belong to their existing owners. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-art))
- **mgd-asset-integration**: Import or update MGD image sources, inspect exports, or connect images to game presentation. Use current tooling and separate candidate previews from runtime replacement; visual creation and new gameplay behavior retain their existing owners. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-asset-integration))
- **mgd-build-release**: Create an MGD APK/web test build, deliver an artifact or execute an assigned release through existing build/CI paths. Verify source identity, runtime mode and Android update conditions. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-build-release))
- **mgd-gameplay**: Implement approved MGD flight, collectible, delivery, scoring or encounter/pacing changes, especially interactions across game systems. Individual hazard behavior uses mgd-hazards; presentation-only UI and performance investigation use their own guidance. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-gameplay))
- **mgd-hazards**: Create or change MGD hazards: movement, spawn, collision, lifecycle or artwork affecting visual fairness. Leads individual hazard requests; encounter-wide rules use mgd-gameplay and standalone image preparation uses the asset workflow. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-hazards))
- **mgd-prototype**: Answer an open MGD design question through a bounded, comparable experiment using existing studies where suitable. Use for exploratory variants and evidence synthesis; specified game implementation and technical Workshop maintenance have separate owners. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-prototype))
- **mgd-structure-interface-audit**: Audit Boltz-2 molecular-glue complex predictions at chain and interface level using raw confidence, uncertainty, deterministic geometry, and optional MGBench reference metrics. Use for structural plausibility and failure analysis; do not infer affinity, cooperativity, ubiquitination, or cellular degradation. ([ohmanbo/rmy](https://github.com/ohmanbo/rmy/tree/HEAD/src/agents/skills/mgd-structure-interface-audit))
- **mgd-tripartite-evidence-review**: Integrate molecular-glue mechanism topology, primary literature, Boltz-2/MGBench structure evidence, deterministic geometry, H4 candidate inference, and cellular context into an evidence-grounded suitability review. Use for end-to-end MGD candidate interpretation; do not use training metrics or predicted structures as experimental proof. ([ohmanbo/rmy](https://github.com/ohmanbo/rmy/tree/HEAD/src/agents/skills/mgd-tripartite-evidence-review))
- **mgd-ui**: Create or change MGD game HUD, menus, buttons, results or Director controls and diagnostics attached to the game. Use existing UI, input and layout ownership. Workshop web shell, catalog, search, storage and import/export use the Workshop workflow; art and game rules use their owners. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-ui))
- **mgd-workshop**: Maintain the MGD Workshop web shell, catalogue/details, search/navigation, local data, validated import/export, sharelinks and technical study registration. Use for Workshop application work; game UI and open design experiments have separate owners. ([bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery](https://github.com/bongohorse/monster-girl-delivery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mgd-workshop))
- **mgear-import-to-scene**: Import asset files into Maya scene with mGear rigging context awareness, following the dcc_mcp_core.asset_import contract. ([dcc-mcp/dcc-mcp-maya-mgear](https://github.com/dcc-mcp/dcc-mcp-maya-mgear/tree/HEAD/skill/mgear-import-to-scene) · ★5)

Reconstruye el cambio (conversación, archivo abierto, diff de Git), inventaría todo el producto y construye el mapa de impacto: notebooks consumidores, funciones compartidas, comentarios, headers, docstrings, README, diccionarios JSON, DDL, metadata de Unity Catalog, YAML de jobs y Declarative Automation Bundles (databricks.yml). Corrige las inconsistencias inequívocas, ejecuta validaciones reales (JSON, YAML, sintaxis Python/PySpark, referencias de bun… ([luisdgo2000/mgeller-change-integrity](https://github.com/luisdgo2000/mgeller-change-integrity/tree/HEAD/skills/mgeller-change-integrity))
- **mgf-and-tsv-benchmark-dataset-export**: Use when you need to construct the ground-truth benchmark set for evaluating MS/MS annotation tools: you have filtered GNPS library spectra plus a structure-organism source table and must produce paired .mgf and .tsv files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-and-tsv-benchmark-dataset-export))
- **mgf-file-parsing**: Use when you have raw or MZmine-processed MGF files (containing MS/MS spectra with m/z values, intensities, and precursor masses) that need to be segmented by sample, deduplicated, or prepared for fragment annotation and adduct analysis in the MolNotator pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mgf-file-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **mgf-file-parsing**: Use when you need per-dataset tandem mass spectrum counts from MGF files, e.g. to reproduce or verify a compareMS2 quality-control figure or sanity-check that MGF conversion preserved all spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-file-parsing))
- **mgf-format-parsing**: Use when input consists of raw MS/MS peak lists in mixed vendor-specific formats that must be normalized to MGF before central database storage or downstream ms_lims processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-format-parsing))
- **mgf-metadata-completion-from-smiles**: Use when when processing MGF-format MS2 spectral libraries (e.g., GNPS) that contain SMILES but lack the Molecular Formula field, and you need to prepare the library for MS-DIAL import or polarity-based separation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mgf-metadata-completion-from-smiles) · 3 copies)
- **mgf-ms2-spectra-loading-and-filtering**: Use when input is .mgf MS2 spectra per sample (e.g. a GNPS/FBMN job export) and you need retention-time-agnostic sample fingerprints from counted peaks and losses instead of RT-aligned feature tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-ms2-spectra-loading-and-filtering))
- **mgf-msms-data-qc-validation**: Use when preparing MGF files and feature tables from MZmine2 pre-processing as QIIME 2 artifacts, before compute-fragmentation-trees, and when checking that make-hierarchy outputs (rooted Phylogeny, filtered hashed table, feature data columns) are correctly formed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msms-data-qc-validation))
- **mgf-msms-feature-import-qiime2**: Use when starting a Qemistree/QIIME 2 metabolomics workflow with pre-processed LC-MS/MS feature data: you have an MGF file (MS1 + MS2 peaks) from feature detection and need it as a QIIME 2 artifact to run SIRIUS/ZODIAC/CSI:FingerID steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msms-feature-import-qiime2))
- **mgf-msp-library-format-conversion**: Use when building an MS-DIAL identification library from multiple public tandem-MS sources in mixed formats (msp and mgf) that need polarity separation and structure-field harmonization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msp-library-format-conversion))
- **mgf-parameter-block-extraction**: Use when the input is a text-format mass spectrometry peak list file in MGF format (or a format jmzReader can export to MGF) and you need structured per-spectrum records with parameters like TITLE, PEPMASS, CHARGE, RTINSECONDS and peak arrays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-parameter-block-extraction))
- **mgf-spectra-conversion-and-database-searching**: Use when starting from vendor raw MS/MS files of immunopeptidomics (or similar enzymatic-digestion-free) experiments that must be converted to MGF and searched with multiple engines before Percolator-based rescoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-spectra-conversion-and-database-searching))
- **mgf-spectra-export**: Use when starting from Thermo RAW (or mzML/mzXML) LC-HRMS2 DDA files that must be converted into a feature quantification table plus per-feature MS2 .mgf spectra for each sample individually, before annotation or molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-export--ac63dc295294572d))
- **mgf-spectra-export**: Use when a Spectra::Spectra object has been filtered or normalized (e.g., via filterSpectriPy/normalize_intensities) and the resulting peak lists must be written out as an MGF file for storage or downstream annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-export--0a5742569b420698))
- **mgf-spectra-file-handling**: Use when LC-MS/MS acquisitions (e.g. serum or food/feed samples) are exported as MGF files and the goal is direct tandem-spectrum comparison — phylogenetics, species identification, or dataset QC — before any database search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-file-handling))
- **mgf-spectra-file-parsing**: Use when an NPLinker-based integrative omics workflow needs MS/MS spectra read from a GNPS molecular networking MGF file, e.g. as the input for CANOPUS class prediction on spectra below 850 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-file-parsing))
- **mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering**: Use when assembling experimental MS/MS benchmarking sets from GNPS or third-party .mgf spectral libraries, and noisy, oversized, or adduct-mixed spectra must be normalized before annotation-tool evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering))
- **mgf-spectra-handling**: Use when MS2 peak data lives as a Python object (e.g., a matchms.Spectrum loaded from MGF) but the analysis runs in R with Spectra, and you need lazy, on-demand access to peaks without duplicating data across languages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-handling))
- **mgf-spectra-import**: Use when MS2 spectra were produced by an external feature-based tool (MZmine, MS-DIAL) exported as MGF/MSP files and must be loaded into R as a Spectra object for downstream matchSpectra() annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-import))
- **mgf-spectra-noise-filtering-and-preprocessing**: Use when raw MGF tandem MS/MS spectra contain low-abundance noise peaks or crowded m/z regions and need deterministic cleaning (intensity and m/z cutoffs, 3-Da de-clustering) before pair-based spectral similarity modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-noise-filtering-and-preprocessing))
- **mgf-spectra-parsing**: Use when you have MGF peak lists of tandem MS2 spectra (e.g. from ProteomeXchange PXD034932) and need pairwise spectral similarities or the compareMS2 set-level distance S/D between datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-parsing))
- **mgf-spectra-reading**: Use when the input is an MGF peak list file (e.g. iPRG2012.mgf) whose spectra must be loaded into Python for downstream processing or benchmarking with spectrum_utils, pymzML, or pyOpenMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-reading))
- **mgf-spectral-data-handling**: Use when input spectra are provided as MGF files (e.g., experimental metabolomics runs or reference libraries) and must be converted into matchms spectrum objects for library-search scoring, filtering, or annotation counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-handling))
- **mgf-spectral-data-import**: Use when raw mass spectra in .mgf (or mzML/mzXML) format must be loaded and converted into a feature table before correlation-based adduct/in-source fragment detection with DBDIpy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-import--7f98239f2a475976))
- **mgf-spectral-data-import**: Use when the input is a .mgf file of MS/MS feature spectra (unique feature identifier, precursor m/z, m/z–intensity tuples) that must be loaded into Python and preprocessed before specXplore similarity or dashboard work. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-import--dff32ae14fa2c1ab))
- **mgf-spectral-file-preparation**: Use when you have ms1-id-derived pseudo MS/MS spectra from full-scan LC-MS (e.g. HILIC-positive mzML from Metabolomics Workbench PR000639) that must be reformatted into a cleaned MGF for GNPS2 molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-file-preparation))
- **mgf-spectral-file-processing**: Use when HR-MS/MS inference spectra or spectral libraries are supplied as MGF files and must be loaded, SMARTS-filtered into 'relevant'/'other' sets, or exported before classifier training/prediction in AnnoMe. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-file-processing))
- **mgf-spectrum-batch-search**: Use when you have a single .mgf file of consensus MS/MS spectra (e.g., from MZmine molecular networking) and need to determine which spectra match microbial metabolomes in GNPS/MassIVE-indexed data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-batch-search))
- **mgf-spectrum-block-segmentation**: Use when the input is a text-format MGF (Mascot Generic Format) peak list file that must be segmented into individual spectra with parameter metadata and (m/z, intensity) peak lists, e.g. for downstream mzIdentML-referenced spectrum lookups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-spectrum-block-segmentation))
- **mgf-spectrum-field-parsing**: Use when input is an MGF tandem mass spectrometry file whose spectra must be parsed into FIDDLE's required 'precursor_mz' and 'precursor_type' fields before candidate formula generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-field-parsing))
- **mgf-spectrum-file-formatting**: Use when pseudo MS/MS spectra have been generated from MS1-only LC-MS or MS imaging data and need to be written as MGF entries containing precursor feature m/z, peaks, and retention time/pixel metadata for annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-file-formatting))
- **mgf-spectrum-format-generation**: Use when raw vendor MS/MS files (e.g. from PRIDE or MassIVE antibody data sets) must be reformatted into an open, text-based spectral format as input for de novo peptide sequencing or database-search tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-format-generation))
- **mgf-spectrum-import**: Use when a workflow starts from an MGF-formatted MS/MS file (e.g. all_your_spectrums.mgf or tests/testdata/pesticides.mgf) and spectra must be loaded into matchms Spectrum objects before applying default_filters or process_peaks pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-import))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when input is an MGF-formatted MS/MS spectrum file (e.g. pesticides.mgf or all_your_spectrums.mgf) and the goal is to load it into matchms Spectrum objects for downstream filtering or scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-parsing--a3a1fb698628055a))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when deconvoluted GC-MS compound spectra are supplied as .mgf (from MSHub auto-deconvolution or another tool) and need to be parsed and annotated against GNPS GC-MS library standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-parsing--9229708bde509438))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when clustering large-scale MS/MS data where raw MGF files must be converted into numeric peak arrays and preprocessed, since peak information occupies over 95% of raw-file data volume and preprocessing is a critical bottleneck contributing 20-90% of overall runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-parsing--c2233cc5c4f3b90d))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when tandem MS/MS data are provided as MGF peak lists (e.g. from a ProteomeXchange/PRIDE deposit such as PXD034932) and spectra must be read with precursor m/z for direct spectral comparison or parameter estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-parsing--4f8205592dee66ad))

([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mgfrantz-roc-auc-macro) · ★3)

- **mgin-dev-skill**: 基于 github.com/maczh/mgin jh/v1 源码与项目文档，开发、审查、排障和扩展 MGIN 微服务，包括启动配置、MVC/DAO、服务发现调用、治理容错、可观测性、消息和脚手架。用户明确指定 MGIN 或维护其依赖时使用；不要把 v2 API 或业务服务约定泛化到 v1。 ([maczh/mgin](https://github.com/maczh/mgin/tree/HEAD/skills/Codex/mgin-dev-skill) · ★6)

- **mhxy-skills**: 梦幻西游技能相关知识库。涵盖生活技能（烹饪、炼药、裁缝等）、装备特技、打造缝纫与炼金、剧情技能、暗器合成等技能系统。当用户询问梦幻西游的技能学习、装备特技、打造炼金、生活技能等内容时使用此skill。 ([MikiVision/xyq-skills](https://github.com/MikiVision/xyq-skills/tree/HEAD/mhxy-skills) · ★28)
- **mhxy-system**: 梦幻西游系统介绍知识库。涵盖聊天社交、交易摆摊、帮派系统、婚姻系统、家园住房、法宝坐骑、结拜图鉴换装等游戏系统。当用户询问梦幻西游的帮派、婚姻、交易、住房、法宝等系统玩法时使用此skill。 ([MikiVision/xyq-skills](https://github.com/MikiVision/xyq-skills/tree/HEAD/mhxy-system) · ★28)
- **mhxy-task**: 梦幻西游任务介绍知识库。涵盖新手任务、师门任务、押镖、捉鬼、官职任务、种族任务、任务链、百晓任务、神器任务、帮派四堂任务及剧情副本等内容。当用户询问梦幻西游的日常任务、帮派任务、剧情副本等内容时使用此skill。 ([MikiVision/xyq-skills](https://github.com/MikiVision/xyq-skills/tree/HEAD/mhxy-task) · ★28)
- **mhxy-tools**: 梦幻西游辅助工具知识库。涵盖梦幻精灵、将军令、保险加锁、密保锁、时间锁、暂离保护、团队契约、小号绑定、租借仓库、角色交易、玩家改名等辅助功能。当用户询问梦幻西游的安全工具、账号保护、角色交易等辅助功能时使用此skill。 ([MikiVision/xyq-skills](https://github.com/MikiVision/xyq-skills/tree/HEAD/mhxy-tools) · ★28)
